# Owner content replacement map

Updated 4 October 2026. This map records the copy applied to the local website. Sources: the owner tables A-H in the attached `Pasted text.txt`, plus the later user-supplied contact details and parent-brand relationship. Owner-supplied business statements are not independently verified certificates or legal findings. Editorial connective copy explains the supplied workflow without adding new metrics or attributed quotes.

## Component scope

- All eight routes and top-level page sections are retained.
- Following the user’s later instructions, Fabrics has eight individual cards; techniques have fifteen individual cards. The existing card grid, image proportions, typography and visual language are retained, with aligned footers and additional rows.
- OEM Journey has six cards. Material selection, sample making and approval are combined in Step 3. The desktop grid has three columns; responsive cards use two columns and switch to one below 381 px for readable narrow-screen text.
- Audits remain text-only. No audit logos or PDFs were added. Existing customer logos were retained as part of the current design; their publication permissions remain to be confirmed.

## Primary field mapping

- [Home / Fact 01 / Header] -> "Since 1998 / พ.ศ. 2541"
- [Home / Fact 02 / Header] -> "Approx. 20M pieces"
- [Home / Fact 03 / Header] -> "50,000 pcs per month"
- [Home / Fact 04 / Header] -> "60 team members"
- [About / Factory summary / Total facility area] -> "Approx. 1,500 sqm"
- [About / Fabrics / Card titles] -> "Cotton", "Woven fabric", "Twill fabric", "Knit fabric", "Sports fabric", "Ottoman fabric", "Stretch fabric", "Rayon"
- [About and Home / Techniques / Card titles] -> "Embroidery", "Rubber screen print", "Water-based print", "DTF printing", "Sublimation", "Garment washing", "Pleating", "Decorative stitching", "Decorative trims", "Elastic application", "Smocking", "Bar tacking", "Pockets & zips", "Buttons & holes", "Garment labels"
- [OEM Journey / Card titles] -> "Brief & reference", "Review & quotation", "Sample & approval", "Bulk production", "Quality control", "Pack & deliver"
- [About / Big C audit / Result] -> "Grade A · 94.61% · Pass"; [Period] -> "2025 assessment"; [Validity] -> "2026 result not supplied"
- [About / Lotus’s audit / Result] -> "Blue Tier"; [Validity] -> "Confirm current audit period"
- [Home, About and OEM Journey / Introduction] -> "Thonburi Master provides garment OEM production through TM Apparel; TM Apparel is part of Thonburi Master." (rendered in Thai)
- [Contact / Factory address; Shared footer / Address] -> "14/160 หมู่บ้านศรีทวีวิลล์ ถนนเอกชัย แขวงบางบอน เขตบางบอน กรุงเทพมหานคร 10150"
- [Contact, Start Your Project and shared Contact block / Phone] -> "02-893-5951–3"; [Fax] -> "02-893-5954"
- [Contact / Monday–Friday and Saturday] -> "08:00–18:00"; [Shared contact fields and footer / Operating hours] -> "Mon–Sat · 08:00–18:00"

## Evidence and uncertainty

- The owner’s “30” for craftspeople’s tenure does not identify whether it is average or maximum. No numerical tenure claim is published.
- Factory figures are placed on the owner’s nominated page: founding, average monthly output, approximate cumulative production and workforce on Home; factory area on About. Repeated founding/output/workforce figures on other pages were replaced.
- The Big C note referred to a late-September 2026 review, but provided no result. The 2025 result is dated explicitly; no 2026 Pass or current-validity claim is inferred. Lotus’s audit period and licence numbers/expiry dates were not supplied.
- GOTS, GRS and RCS are conditional sourcing options for fabrics, not certifications of this factory. Organic/recycled and viscose alternatives depend on specifications, order minimums, suppliers and documentation. Ottoman water repellency is specification-dependent.
- The child-labour statement is attributed to the factory. No claim is made that the audit reports independently prove it because the reports were not supplied.
- Timeline ranges are estimates in days, not guaranteed service levels. No combined end-to-end SLA is invented. Sample-fee credit and order terms should be confirmed with the team.
- Gallery photographs are references, not verified customer cases or batch records. Fabric images are AI-generated illustrations. Factory/people/gallery assets inherited from Figma are not verified photographs of the owner’s actual site, equipment or staff.
- Thonburi Master is the main website identity; TM Apparel is described as part of it. The supplied address, telephone, fax and Monday-Saturday 08:00-18:00 hours replace inherited contact fields everywhere. The Maps link searches the exact Thai address; no coordinates are inferred. Unconfirmed email, LINE and WhatsApp channels were replaced with the supplied phone/fax/hours/visit fields. Formal registered company name, customer-logo rights, complete policies and enquiry delivery remain unresolved.

## Images

Eight individual fabric images are saved in `public/media/fabrics/`, with an additional cotton/knit reference on Home. Generated images use the built-in imagegen tool. Exact prompts and workspace asset paths are in [owner-image-prompts.json](owner-image-prompts.json). Captions/alt text identify fabric images as illustrations. Unrelated dance/travel project visuals and fictional executive portraits have been replaced with material, garment and production references.

## Complete field mapping

Selectors identify the rendered field exactly. Character counts compare normalised source strings; they are a review aid, not proof of visual fit. Additional individual cards have no original placeholder. Where six journey cards absorb eight old stages, positional comparisons are not semantic one-to-one replacements; use the primary journey mapping above. Repeated shared-component fields may appear once.

### About Us / about-hero

- [About Us / about-hero / text / `html:nth-of-type(1) > body:nth-of-type(1) > main:nth-of-type(1) > section:nth-of-type(1) > div:nth-of-type(1) > div:nth-of-type(1) > p:nth-of-type(1)`] -> "Thonburi Master · TM Apparel" (characters: 16 -> 28; source: Owner A-H where relevant; editorial clarification)
- [About Us / about-hero / text / `html:nth-of-type(1) > body:nth-of-type(1) > main:nth-of-type(1) > section:nth-of-type(1) > div:nth-of-type(1) > div:nth-of-type(1) > p:nth-of-type(2)`] -> "TM Apparel เป็นส่วนหนึ่งของ Thonburi Master ให้บริการผลิตเสื้อผ้า OEM สำหรับแบรนด์ไทยและต่างประเทศ ตั้งแต่รับบรีฟ ประเมินรายละเอียด เลือกวัสดุ พัฒนาตัวอย่าง ผลิตจริง ตรวจคุณภาพ จนถึงส่งมอบ เราวางแผนงานร่วมกับลูกค้าและใช้ตัวอย่างที่อนุมัติเป็นแนวทางในการผลิต เพื่อให้ขนาด รูปทรง และรายละเอียดสอดคล้องกับความต้องการของแต่ละแบรนด์" (characters: 311 -> 327; source: Owner A-H where relevant; editorial clarification)
- [About Us / about-hero / text / `html:nth-of-type(1) > body:nth-of-type(1) > main:nth-of-type(1) > section:nth-of-type(1) > div:nth-of-type(1) > div:nth-of-type(1) > p:nth-of-type(3)`] -> "เราวางแผนงานร่วมกับลูกค้าตั้งแต่เลือกผ้าและวัสดุ จนถึงอนุมัติตัวอย่าง ทีมตัด เย็บ และรีดประชุมเปิดแบบร่วมกันก่อนเริ่มผลิต เพื่อให้ขนาด รูปทรง เทคนิค และจุดตรวจคุณภาพเป็นไปตามรายละเอียดที่ตกลง" (characters: 186 -> 191; source: Owner A-H where relevant; editorial clarification)

QC and packing/delivery each take about 2–5 days. These estimates are not a combined delivery guarantee." (characters: 88 -> 230; source: B/D and owner contact/identity update; editorial connective copy)
